── what this run does and does not show ────────────
All native fixtures used here carry the composited material (materialRendered: true).
the shape axis is BOUNDED to the declared component region (margin 0 device px): within it, coverage, contour and corner profile are measured as before; outside it nothing is recovered, so a surface drawn LARGER than its declaration reads as a match. Area recovery is assumed, not measured — read silhouetteArea{Native,Web} against componentRegionArea.
shadow axis NOT NORMALISED on 3 cell(s) across 3 scene(s): dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive, impulse__rrect-lg__inactive, impulse__rrect-ml__inactive. Those backdrops carry too little light for an occlusion ratio to exist; the absolute departure is still reported and every ratio is absent, not zero.
caveat: Profiles not captured because the system is in accessibility mode 'standard' and these claim another: apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-reduced-transparency, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-increased-contrast. macOS exposes reduce-transparency and increase-contrast as read-only environment values, so each such profile needs its own run with the corresponding System Settings toggle on (Accessibility > Display).
caveat: Profiles not captured because this display renders at 2.0x and their keys state another scale: apple-macos-26.5-1x-dark-standard, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-standard. A profile is only captured on a display whose backingScaleFactor matches its key — a mismatched run would file 640x400-pixel fixtures under a key claiming a different scale, with no trace in the bytes.
receded profile posed on the inactive scenes: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-light-standard-glass0.5-receded.json
material profile applied to the web side: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-light-standard-glass0.5.json
web accessibility for apple-macos-27.0-1x-light-standard-glass0.5 (native standard): browser preferences, no overrides

── what this run does and does not show ────────────
All native fixtures used here carry the composited material (materialRendered: true).
the shape axis is BOUNDED to the declared component region (margin 0 device px): within it, coverage, contour and corner profile are measured as before; outside it nothing is recovered, so a surface drawn LARGER than its declaration reads as a match. Area recovery is assumed, not measured — read silhouetteArea{Native,Web} against componentRegionArea.
shadow axis NOT NORMALISED on 3 cell(s) across 3 scene(s): impulse__rrect-lg__inactive, impulse__rrect-ml__inactive, dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive. Those backdrops carry too little light for an occlusion ratio to exist; the absolute departure is still reported and every ratio is absent, not zero.
caveat: Profiles not captured because the system is in accessibility mode 'standard' and these claim another: apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-reduced-transparency, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-increased-contrast. macOS exposes reduce-transparency and increase-contrast as read-only environment values, so each such profile needs its own run with the corresponding System Settings toggle on (Accessibility > Display).
caveat: Profiles not captured because this display renders at 2.0x and their keys state another scale: apple-macos-26.5-1x-dark-standard, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-standard. A profile is only captured on a display whose backingScaleFactor matches its key — a mismatched run would file 640x400-pixel fixtures under a key claiming a different scale, with no trace in the bytes.
receded profile posed on the inactive scenes: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-light-standard-glass0.5-receded.json
material profile applied to the web side: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-light-standard-glass0.5.json
web accessibility for apple-macos-27.0-2x-light-standard-glass0.5 (native standard): browser preferences, no overrides

── what this run does and does not show ────────────
All native fixtures used here carry the composited material (materialRendered: true).
material axis absent on 1 cell(s) across 1 scene(s): dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive. See the per-cell notes — absent means not identifiable on that scene, never zero.
the shape axis is BOUNDED to the declared component region (margin 0 device px): within it, coverage, contour and corner profile are measured as before; outside it nothing is recovered, so a surface drawn LARGER than its declaration reads as a match. Area recovery is assumed, not measured — read silhouetteArea{Native,Web} against componentRegionArea.
shadow axis NOT NORMALISED on 3 cell(s) across 3 scene(s): impulse__rrect-ml__inactive, impulse__rrect-lg__inactive, dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive. Those backdrops carry too little light for an occlusion ratio to exist; the absolute departure is still reported and every ratio is absent, not zero.
caveat: Profiles not captured because the system is in accessibility mode 'standard' and these claim another: apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-reduced-transparency, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-increased-contrast. macOS exposes reduce-transparency and increase-contrast as read-only environment values, so each such profile needs its own run with the corresponding System Settings toggle on (Accessibility > Display).
caveat: Profiles not captured because this display renders at 2.0x and their keys state another scale: apple-macos-26.5-1x-dark-standard, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-standard. A profile is only captured on a display whose backingScaleFactor matches its key — a mismatched run would file 640x400-pixel fixtures under a key claiming a different scale, with no trace in the bytes.

── what this run does and does not show ────────────
All native fixtures used here carry the composited material (materialRendered: true).
material axis absent on 1 cell(s) across 1 scene(s): dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive. See the per-cell notes — absent means not identifiable on that scene, never zero.
the shape axis is BOUNDED to the declared component region (margin 0 device px): within it, coverage, contour and corner profile are measured as before; outside it nothing is recovered, so a surface drawn LARGER than its declaration reads as a match. Area recovery is assumed, not measured — read silhouetteArea{Native,Web} against componentRegionArea.
shadow axis NOT NORMALISED on 3 cell(s) across 3 scene(s): dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive, impulse__rrect-lg__inactive, impulse__rrect-ml__inactive. Those backdrops carry too little light for an occlusion ratio to exist; the absolute departure is still reported and every ratio is absent, not zero.
caveat: Profiles not captured because the system is in accessibility mode 'standard' and these claim another: apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-reduced-transparency, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-increased-contrast. macOS exposes reduce-transparency and increase-contrast as read-only environment values, so each such profile needs its own run with the corresponding System Settings toggle on (Accessibility > Display).
caveat: Profiles not captured because this display renders at 2.0x and their keys state another scale: apple-macos-26.5-1x-dark-standard, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-standard. A profile is only captured on a display whose backingScaleFactor matches its key — a mismatched run would file 640x400-pixel fixtures under a key claiming a different scale, with no trace in the bytes.
receded profile posed on the inactive scenes: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-dark-standard-glass0.5-receded.json
material profile applied to the web side: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-dark-standard-glass0.5.json
web accessibility for apple-macos-27.0-2x-dark-standard-glass0.5 (native standard): browser preferences, no overrides

── what this run does and does not show ────────────
All native fixtures used here carry the composited material (materialRendered: true).
the shape axis is BOUNDED to the declared component region (margin 0 device px): within it, coverage, contour and corner profile are measured as before; outside it nothing is recovered, so a surface drawn LARGER than its declaration reads as a match. Area recovery is assumed, not measured — read silhouetteArea{Native,Web} against componentRegionArea.
shadow axis NOT NORMALISED on 3 cell(s) across 3 scene(s): dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive, impulse__rrect-lg__inactive, impulse__rrect-ml__inactive. Those backdrops carry too little light for an occlusion ratio to exist; the absolute departure is still reported and every ratio is absent, not zero.
coherence axis on 55 of 55 dom cell(s) — this tier against its texture twin, web against web.
caveat: Profiles not captured because the system is in accessibility mode 'standard' and these claim another: apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-reduced-transparency, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-increased-contrast. macOS exposes reduce-transparency and increase-contrast as read-only environment values, so each such profile needs its own run with the corresponding System Settings toggle on (Accessibility > Display).
caveat: Profiles not captured because this display renders at 2.0x and their keys state another scale: apple-macos-26.5-1x-dark-standard, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-standard. A profile is only captured on a display whose backingScaleFactor matches its key — a mismatched run would file 640x400-pixel fixtures under a key claiming a different scale, with no trace in the bytes.
receded profile posed on the inactive scenes: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-light-standard-glass0.5-receded.json
material profile applied to the web side: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-light-standard-glass0.5.json
web accessibility for apple-macos-27.0-1x-light-standard-glass0.5 (native standard): browser preferences, no overrides

── what this run does and does not show ────────────
All native fixtures used here carry the composited material (materialRendered: true).
material axis absent on 1 cell(s) across 1 scene(s): dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive. See the per-cell notes — absent means not identifiable on that scene, never zero.
the shape axis is BOUNDED to the declared component region (margin 0 device px): within it, coverage, contour and corner profile are measured as before; outside it nothing is recovered, so a surface drawn LARGER than its declaration reads as a match. Area recovery is assumed, not measured — read silhouetteArea{Native,Web} against componentRegionArea.
shadow axis NOT NORMALISED on 3 cell(s) across 3 scene(s): impulse__rrect-ml__inactive, impulse__rrect-lg__inactive, dark-solid__rrect-lg__inactive. Those backdrops carry too little light for an occlusion ratio to exist; the absolute departure is still reported and every ratio is absent, not zero.
coherence axis on 55 of 55 dom cell(s) — this tier against its texture twin, web against web.
caveat: Profiles not captured because the system is in accessibility mode 'standard' and these claim another: apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-reduced-transparency, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-increased-contrast. macOS exposes reduce-transparency and increase-contrast as read-only environment values, so each such profile needs its own run with the corresponding System Settings toggle on (Accessibility > Display).
caveat: Profiles not captured because this display renders at 2.0x and their keys state another scale: apple-macos-26.5-1x-dark-standard, apple-macos-26.5-1x-light-standard. A profile is only captured on a display whose backingScaleFactor matches its key — a mismatched run would file 640x400-pixel fixtures under a key claiming a different scale, with no trace in the bytes.
receded profile posed on the inactive scenes: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-dark-standard-glass0.5-receded.json
material profile applied to the web side: /Users/new/Developer/GitHub/designer/.claude/worktrees/w32-g1-shadow-fit/packages/calibration/profiles/apple-macos-27.0-1x-dark-standard-glass0.5.json
web accessibility for apple-macos-27.0-1x-dark-standard-glass0.5 (native standard): browser preferences, no overrides
